# Changelog

## [0.32.0](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/compare/v0.31.0...v0.32.0) (2026-11-02)


### Features

* **html:** a lazy image waits to be near the viewport, where every image was asked for as it was read ([#1177](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/1177)) ([afe789e](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/afe789ed0bbd89f940a4a75b7e0e853ab039c050))
* **html:** a page's scrubbed VP9 clip plays on the system's decoder and its WebGL draws on the GPU, where a MediaSource turned every type down and a context drew nothing ([#1214](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/1214)) ([72bac3b](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/72bac3bc29daa870918ef968b4ee5d4fcbbbc3d3))
* **html:** crypto.subtle and CryptoKey for a page's scripts, where subtle was undefined and a tool that asked for it stopped there ([#1236](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/1236)) ([1e8b09c](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/1e8b09c30c1b142aac27ee32f1e0245d69dda73b))
* **html:** decodeAudioData reads WAV, where every sound a page asked to decode was an EncodingError ([#1233](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/1233)) ([d69169a](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/d69169aca981b99c9ec7fea9a1f9e8702815d839))
* **html:** each SVG element has its interface and a drawing says where what it holds is, where getBBox answered the box of the whole svg ([#1228](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/1228)) ([9b2ed33](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/9b2ed33e2f510ce78b09ede6e4c65721ccc146fa))
* **html:** execCommand, AbortSignal.any, scheduler, navigator.permissions and XMLHttpRequestEventTarget, where a page that asked for one threw at its first line ([#1226](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/1226)) ([0cf4ee3](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/0cf4ee3bce2f0c79aaa829b7e3a9bb782a4f6391))
* **html:** new FontFace loads a font from a URL or a buffer and document.fonts takes it, where the constructor did not exist ([#1227](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/1227)) ([27109cf](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/27109cfa77454f9907eb7be381ee9457cce3883c))


### Bug Fixes

* **html:** a ::before or an ::after of display: contents puts its content in what is around it, where each was a block of its own ([#1198](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/1198)) ([b935f38](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/b935f3858f81952197186a6a533b788fe8f4fdf6))
* **html:** a ::before with a box reads its used size and edges, and a margin what layout gave it, where it read as computed and a margin was worked out again from the parent ([#1202](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/1202)) ([dc541ff](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/dc541ffdd2cedc70feead02f6d6e28a1549f2179))
* **html:** a :has() follows the fragment as :target does, where it kept what it first answered through every fragment after it ([#1158](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/1158)) ([83b38c0](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/83b38c09230448d67be947fba565240d72019f3e))
* **html:** a &lt;link&gt; or a &lt;base&gt; the parser made in a drawing is no HTML element, where it was read as a sheet and as the document's base ([#1232](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/1232)) ([a39ac2d](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/a39ac2d66ee0a9f7ee0ec70db467f6e8a9b59384))
* **html:** a body past the limit is a failed request and the limit is 256 MB, where one past 16 MiB was handed on cut short ([#1223](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/1223)) ([a3bc225](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/a3bc225e841e0af994a3aab509ef531b6c85b366))
* **html:** a box no frame holds is laid out level whatever else is on the page, where a frame elsewhere set its text for a paint it never had ([#1166](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/1166)) ([2378ec0](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/2378ec0a038e8b396c5bcf3ff34fbfe47dbdedfb))
* **html:** a change outside an inline drawing reaches what a rule gives its shapes, where they stayed as they were until something built the box ([#1218](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/1218)) ([b2307c1](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/b2307c1d253f085a4cb277f642635ac3587625b7))
* **html:** a change to a radio's group reaches the fieldset around each radio in it, where a change outside the fieldset left its answer as it was ([#1194](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/1194)) ([6344cb2](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/6344cb21d4a9c3c695d519a56df26c97dfada0f4))
* **html:** a computed style answers its shorthands, margin and gap and border among them, where a page read undefined for all but a few ([#1212](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/1212)) ([60f001e](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/60f001eafcb2d4aa4c922a2599e59065716d3d15))
* **html:** a connection asks only the STUN servers its page named, and a data channel closes once what it sent has arrived ([#1154](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/1154)) ([c04b772](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/c04b772c677fbff8929e374b0730ee6f5bc37cfd))
* **html:** a display: contents element's ::before and ::after are items of the flex or grid box around it, where the two went inline in one anonymous block ([#1195](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/1195)) ([a85afb1](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/a85afb124b9d0625e088878b3ac64a54fee09b9e))
* **html:** a document with a WebTransport session open is kept and the session ends, where it was held out of the cache as a WebSocket's is ([#1183](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/1183)) ([cfab255](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/cfab2551bdd28997fed47a5bfb0a3a86024e2ac3))
* **html:** a flex box comes to its narrowest as Chromium has it, where a probe read each item at the width it was laid out at ([#1197](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/1197)) ([2aecf56](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/2aecf560c3197359cbf530345f139501ed3637ae))
* **html:** a flex box comes to its widest as Chromium has it, where a layout read each item at its content ([#1203](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/1203)) ([33d4598](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/33d4598b061defb730fd329540dc30b3c5e56909))
* **html:** a flex item's auto margin reads the room it came to, where Yoga reports none and it read 0px ([#1205](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/1205)) ([e405e5f](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/e405e5fc9a636eb5a764954f47ee83af79bbe8b3))
* **html:** a frame or a video in a sticky box stays with the box as the pane scrolls, where it went with the text and was put back at the paint ([#1182](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/1182)) ([44efb7f](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/44efb7fda2e44f5f61478ff1ca29ec18d130ce76))
* **html:** a frame or a video in a sticky box under a scale stays with the box as the pane scrolls, where it was still a frame late ([#1185](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/1185)) ([ad19e35](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/ad19e357074540628c43e58ef067525440bcde52))
* **html:** a grid's tracks come to what Chromium has them come to, where an item gave its content and no row reached a ratio ([#1217](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/1217)) ([7744294](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/7744294cb62a6797c10a7d75f84bb619dbf19b55))
* **html:** a kept block's lines move with the text, where one that opens with an image kept the places it had ([#1181](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/1181)) ([8129e30](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/8129e306892628d305afba411cb0ce6ff8614f84))
* **html:** a kept document's workers stop with it, where one could open a connection nobody would see and the document stayed kept ([#1179](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/1179)) ([b713bf4](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/b713bf428f4279ef9c1560476fd81ab873e50f5f))
* **html:** a logical property whose var() has no value goes back as though unset, where it kept what an earlier declaration gave it ([#1191](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/1191)) ([6646d4c](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/6646d4c24ac44e967faf43c9f5b3da590ac5cb86))
* **html:** a longhand goes back alone, where a var() in one corner's radius took the other corners back with it ([#1184](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/1184)) ([2cfcad6](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/2cfcad628a86f30b3676174241f39d4cc14e08a5))
* **html:** a min-width of auto reads as zero but on a flex or a grid item, and a size keyword as the keyword, where both read as auto ([#1192](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/1192)) ([3a783f4](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/3a783f42cf1ae98d928984c0da12de753134fcd5))
* **html:** a NodeList and an HTMLCollection are live lists and no arrays, where each was a snapshot in an Array subclass ([#1225](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/1225)) ([366a166](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/366a166800acc98b394939c383a7d2415720adbe))
* **html:** a page reads an element's font as the shorthand it comes to, where it read nothing and a canvas kept its default ([#1168](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/1168)) ([c1a7a6a](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/c1a7a6a6b9c27b68406e5329776167ba0cdeda55))
* **html:** a page reads the font family it wrote, where it read the names a document's faces go by here ([#1156](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/1156)) ([08b1c6b](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/08b1c6be02216d1a4f21c7625219f5cb8003c130))
* **html:** a rem is the size of the &lt;html&gt; a document does not write, where it was that of an element at its top ([#1200](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/1200)) ([e6ff65a](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/e6ff65a0fa693075831bafc92568d8413d9988f6))
* **html:** a restyle in place reads a container's size from the boxes, where the first to read one answered as though it had none ([#1170](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/1170)) ([2bf3860](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/2bf38602cabd43602104cbf17531d20451397cd0))
* **html:** a rule a sheet adds or a breakpoint turns on reaches the drawings that name what it styles, where they stayed as they were until a refresh of everything ([#1231](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/1231)) ([f35e854](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/f35e854c7bf05d96b72f6c6bb80fcb9fe39f3f3b))
* **html:** a rule on a control's state matches, where css-select refused :invalid and its kin and the rule was dropped with no error ([#1178](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/1178)) ([0e1f15b](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/0e1f15b775d7d1cecabeb709b3d0d1c94277c315))
* **html:** a sheet in a drawing is read when a change to it is told, where what a script wrote to one waited for a refresh that said nothing ([#1221](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/1221)) ([29cf7a5](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/29cf7a5ec1123038c53dd1c100d2e35320a35597))
* **html:** a sticky inline box stands where its lines have it and sticks as a block does, where how many layouts had run said where it stood ([#1160](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/1160)) ([1860d66](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/1860d66afad2e52a17f0c5dc3c0d938f96263dea))
* **html:** a told change to what an inline drawing names repaints the drawing, where it moved no pixel until another paint reached it ([#1222](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/1222)) ([52040d2](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/52040d203faaecff663cbde61fb505071de75c7d))
* **html:** a transform moves a sticky box from where it sticks, where stickiness took it back ([#1171](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/1171)) ([c548b3e](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/c548b3ee9f7a1e48592bbf8f3984190cc569a917))
* **html:** a transform reads none where nothing renders the element, and the frame getKeyframes() makes up reads margins and padding as laid out, where the one read its list and the other stayed a percentage ([#1208](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/1208)) ([9703397](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/9703397f17a8618374fc83727ba2f71613af2b73))
* **html:** a widget in a sticky box stays with the box as the pane scrolls, where it went with the text and was put back at the paint ([#1180](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/1180)) ([57b77fc](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/57b77fca89c9c9ebbe74055d37fc03bb842e93b5))
* **html:** a worker ended as its thread starts waits for its modules, where some Node releases aborted the process ([#1196](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/1196)) ([00038ea](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/00038ea8a03f82995df733d5101a503eabb457de))
* **html:** an absolute box placed against an inline box reads the insets it was placed by, and an empty inline box is the rectangle a browser has, where the sides it did not set read auto and it was as tall as its font ([#1206](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/1206)) ([f12b01d](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/f12b01dd09544f4326cc0dc9cae3edab301dc3c4))
* **html:** an element that is a container keeps what it read of the container above it, where its pseudo-element's read took the place of its own ([#1162](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/1162)) ([ce731c0](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/ce731c0515f4b9166b8cefbc96eb415d92f45aba))
* **html:** an image stretched in a flex row is laid out, where a box of one line had no width to set back to auto ([#1220](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/1220)) ([1f75dd7](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/1f75dd7854aa2869fd307cd603bf46fc477c7288))
* **html:** an inline box an inset moves is painted with the positioned boxes, where it was painted by its line and what came after it in the flow was drawn over it ([#1169](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/1169)) ([abee08f](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/abee08fd239f97f1c530dc01a123a8e69778e2e8))
* **html:** an inline box with nothing in it measures where it stands on its lines, where its text offset found a line of a paragraph above ([#1167](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/1167)) ([2de0cf9](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/2de0cf93c97d4612512a375215e39e8a29c09a41))
* **html:** calc-size() reads as Chromium reads it, and the limits and flex-basis take it, where it read as its keyword and they refused it ([#1204](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/1204)) ([f674f84](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/f674f84e0ae3ee7e6fa3febc3e245afc06d0faa3))
* **html:** font-family takes and refuses what Chrome does, where a keyword in a list was a name and an escape dropped the declaration ([#1172](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/1172)) ([54ea08c](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/54ea08c8d873fc423af3c3f0cd85eb292d9d97ee))
* **html:** getComputedStyle reads the flex and alignment properties as Chromium does, and flex, flex-flow and place-* are refused whole, where most read nothing and each kept what it could read ([#1213](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/1213)) ([7c28850](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/7c288502d7f2e53b285c5be64740c8a8532ac3fa))
* **html:** scroll-margin and scroll-padding have logical names that follow the flow, and place-content goes back, where each stayed as it was ([#1193](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/1193)) ([452e10a](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/452e10ab254c4b2ebd0d86394de4cea353e5a07e))
* **html:** style containment counts alike built apart and whole, where a list's first number turned on whether a counter was around it ([#1165](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/1165)) ([db136d0](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/db136d04d8c0edf430d7cdd95f2269058d49e5e5))

### Performance Improvements

* **html:** a block around a flow in another writing mode is kept, where twelve turned labels laid out a whole table at every layout ([#1159](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/1159)) ([36814d6](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/36814d66f8cb6bbdd4eff962da1542c786239c07))
* **html:** a box is laid out where it stands, where every box around it moved what it keeps to its origin and back ([#1199](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/1199)) ([8c5b000](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/8c5b000258fb2b354e3ec3b2e2669b4d8eb463df))
* **html:** a box that turns walks its own ink, where every frame of a spinner walked the whole document's ([#1186](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/1186)) ([48bdc66](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/48bdc6637d621cfc0aabf66d6b1062b89672bd59))
* **html:** a drag that fell behind holds what a page hears of its viewport, where each width cost a second cycle of its script ([#1210](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/1210)) ([57b56ed](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/57b56ed1104e426076073f5791e30b47180c1210))
* **html:** a face that arrives sets what can be seen, and what nobody can see waits, where each arrival styled, built and laid out the whole document ([#1152](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/1152)) ([200374c](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/200374c3b1ea99cdd2f64148460f94e4af007881))
* **html:** a flex box comes to its narrowest by adding up its items, where a probe laid all of it out at no width ([#1190](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/1190)) ([52041b6](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/52041b648fccdfa91ec951cbed67b4b38d54cb70))
* **html:** a flex box of one line makes no call into Yoga, where every layout of it crossed the bridge a style and an answer at a time ([#1215](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/1215)) ([9691a5d](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/9691a5dcf9829e48ad631a508d2827d55c2f75a3))
* **html:** a layout keeps the order of the layers, where each one walked the tree to work it out again ([#1209](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/1209)) ([52e7064](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/52e7064f4bc8f2b66e028c33b2fdbc70c62cb8ba))
* **html:** a list item reads what its last build read, where a change in one built the whole document ([#1161](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/1161)) ([f319ffa](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/f319ffa4d0ad67d04523b6a2c29ab4c1708873d5))
* **html:** a long row whose items neither grow nor shrink keeps its layout at another width, where every step of a window's edge laid it out again ([#1201](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/1201)) ([bd7e62b](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/bd7e62b49864262facce0c46038a5b1dc0338d54))
* **html:** a page of rows restyles the rows it put in, where one rule reading a direction restyled every row there was ([#1187](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/1187)) ([d760ab8](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/d760ab8d28ec964eea97239b7801a536d4ca8159))
* **html:** a refresh told what changed keeps the inline drawings it did not reach, where each was read again at every paint after it ([#1216](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/1216)) ([52f9f8f](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/52f9f8f486422b658e71889f8185722633310160))
* **html:** a resize lays out what its width reaches, where every step of a window's edge laid out all of the document again ([#1188](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/1188)) ([343f8cc](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/343f8cc04d24ee894c16567ff79814bdf699244c))

## [0.31.0](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/compare/v0.30.1...v0.31.0) (2026-10-31)


### Features

* **html:** navigator.platform is the one the user agent names, and the example browser names the OS it runs on ([#910](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/910)) ([05f9a9f](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/05f9a9fb2a8dabf358d12dccf79b44ce0ec54d5c))

### Performance Improvements

* **html:** a cascade made again takes the sheets the one before parsed ([#949](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/949)) ([2a23eca](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/2a23eca0d084b07d976675c9a8a2c8fa6ca1f78f))

* **html:** an image generated content names builds the boxes again with every style kept ([#947](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/947)) ([9fcf6d0](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/9fcf6d01a03a18b346463b26b0df0b736a56576a))
* **html:** two custom property sets are compared once a pair ([#948](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/948)) ([5c19d8f](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/5c19d8faf11e8f78039313d6b9591b16543acbdc))

## [0.26.0](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/compare/v0.25.0...v0.26.0) (2026-10-21)


### Features

* **examples:** the browser gives the page the whole screen in full screen, its strip and toolbar back at the top edge or while the address bar has the focus ([#823](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/823)) ([ab32e62](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/ab32e62a74536f0186cda84c278bde1ef7309678))

### Bug Fixes

## [0.25.0](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/compare/v0.24.0...v0.25.0) (2026-10-20)


### Features

* **html:** [@property](https://github.com/property) registers a custom property, so one nothing sets has its initial value and one that does not inherit starts again under what sets it ([#809](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/809)) ([9c1cfaa](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/9c1cfaac03568a70045b988d8ee2d84593e2643b))

* **html:** a page's scripts define custom elements, so the React apps GitHub starts from a &lt;react-app&gt; run and github.com/features draws what they reveal ([#787](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/787)) ([6c62a5b](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/6c62a5b76f912d7ba4d3c4f09b03824269b3b9c5))
