* **html:** offsetParent and the offsets are measured from the offset parent's padding edge, and a custom property keeps its case, so Primer's tab strip slides its pill under the tab pressed ([#788](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/788)) ([80c36a3](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/80c36a30c60f9b86b09f80060337d97aaf6fa36f))
* **html:** repeating-linear-gradient and repeating-radial-gradient are drawn, so a card's holographic sheen and the light on its edge show ([#811](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/811)) ([0f94f62](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/0f94f62c63b3872c43a59e4f3ac6ba22cc479163))
* **html:** showModal puts a dialog in the top layer, so :modal matches it, it is drawn centred over the whole page with its ::backdrop under it, and Escape and close take it out ([#801](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/801)) ([b72fb0b](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/b72fb0bb4bf1d047a000ed709913132ced26273e))
* **html:** subgrids, so a row of cards each a subgrid down its grid's rows has every card's heading, price and button on one line, as GitHub's pricing cards are written to ([#802](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/802)) ([abe2844](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/abe28447fca5e2191cacba14ef7edb717705b208))


### Bug Fixes

* **charts:** a line, an area or a bar envelope wider than any screen walks only the pixel columns the window shows, so a plot billions of pixels wide does not hang its paint ([#815](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/815)) ([b5b9790](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/b5b97902f1772ca98bfa728d25fea558ee494bc3))
* **charts:** a scatter laid out larger than any screen bins only what the window shows, so a page core lays out billions of pixels tall draws nothing rather than running the process out of memory ([#814](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/814)) ([cbd88d4](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/cbd88d434cd5d961f6ed95f463bf900c5562caaf))
* **flow:** the GL renderer puts back the vertex array it found bound, so a 3D canvas drawn after a flow on macOS draws its scene and not one point ([#816](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/816)) ([a160833](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/a1608330b155283e2dc0450e0ffb70c8e2c62c36))
* **html:** -webkit-line-clamp cuts its last line inside a word where the ellipsis has no room, as Chrome, Firefox and WebKit cut it ([#813](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/813)) ([8b0536b](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/8b0536b95ce1d45b9e807ed139ea0ed63f835581))
* **html:** a :has() and an attribute selector restyle only what their answer changed for, and a sheet's element or a stylesheet prop that reads the same restyles nothing, so github.com/features stops styling itself whole inside its scripts ([#791](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/791)) ([c9ca770](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/c9ca770847fa8c0e0fcec16756a84f42481ca1fb))
* **html:** a box fixed to the viewport is measured where the pane's scroll draws it, so a page's getBoundingClientRect reads a fixed header where it is and a Tab into it scrolls nothing ([#792](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/792)) ([25c15ba](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/25c15ba5ce5118c814f5c4ce2de323fe3bdd4207))
* **html:** a box fixed to the viewport that draws nothing is no rect a scroll repaints, so a page with a contents list stuck at the top scrolls with a copy rather than repainting the window ([#796](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/796)) ([35b805b](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/35b805bdeeaf781c90b2fc950290322f32b50e7f))
* **html:** a box the document paints over a form control cuts the control's widget, so a menu opened over a form is no longer drawn under the form's fields ([#806](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/806)) ([787c585](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/787c5850672b22638905eba8d9cb90e46bfbf866))
* **html:** a closed dialog is not drawn, and a box fixed to the viewport stays where the viewport has it inside a sticky or a relative box, so docs.github.com's search dialog no longer sits over its breadcrumbs and runs down the window as the page scrolls ([#800](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/800)) ([72ab5a0](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/72ab5a0484a230d8d7932c37f78c7cb1eb6abe22))
* **html:** a form control in a box fixed to the viewport keeps its widget where the box is drawn, so a fixed header's search field stays in the header as the page scrolls ([#794](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/794)) ([5e0cb9f](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/5e0cb9f216fdf042458f604f7ed414ac77228443))
* **html:** a header cell takes its row's text-align where that is set, as HTML's rendering rules have it, so github.blog's table headers sit at the left ([#784](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/784)) ([16eeb29](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/16eeb297d3a7bd8026b21cbc61cc45e4516fed79))
* **html:** a menu a page's script opens through ariaHidden is shown, and a click under GitHub's delegated-events no longer throws on the document's tagName, so github.blog's header menus open ([#781](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/781)) ([f3ee6b8](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/f3ee6b8951be9f4d5f044f915a0d27e6f38992e2))
* **html:** a page measures a box fixed to the viewport where its own scrollTo has it, before the pane is laid out again, so a header fixed at top: 30px reads 30 straight after scrollTo(0, 500) ([#795](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/795)) ([c29755a](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/c29755adeb121867adeedf3fb1253d6140a57e09))
* **html:** a page that adopted a sheet hands the element the same sheets render after render, so a change its scripts make restyles what it reaches and not the whole document ([#799](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/799)) ([1b89fda](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/1b89fda31ecad778464d625668e9caf4fd9b7a3b))
* **html:** a rule at a shadow tree's edge no longer makes every change to the document a restyle of all of it, so GitHub's pages keep up with their own scripts ([#786](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/786)) ([9bec5d6](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/9bec5d6fe9754b1016866f5037f7abbc0a531098))
* **html:** a sticky box follows the viewport as the pane around the document scrolls it, so github.blog's table of contents stays under its header ([#779](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/779)) ([4e062c3](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/4e062c32ccba32f6647b8035683de0ac984d4c3b))
* **html:** a sticky box keeps inside its containing block by its own bottom margin, so github.blog's table of contents runs to the end of its sidebar ([#783](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/783)) ([ddc471a](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/ddc471a9ea85a8f4fd1e061c3c179b8a04eb162e))
* **html:** a sticky box the viewport holds is measured where a page's own scrollTo has it, before the pane is laid out again, so a header held at top: 0 reads 0 straight after scrollTo(0, 500) ([#798](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/798)) ([95ca13a](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/95ca13aebcffbc50bdac35b2ea8f15ee9b1d8713))
* **html:** a video plays the &lt;source&gt; its media chooses, and what the page paints over it is painted over its player, so GitHub's hero plays under its play button ([#789](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/789)) ([64c5d99](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/64c5d99001ed2ad42560daa597a5b35112336fba))
* **html:** an &lt;html&gt; or a &lt;head&gt; inside the document is no element, as HTML's parser has it, so github.blog's article tables span their column ([#782](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/782)) ([8e2d8a5](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/8e2d8a55cb9c6e3912fb75d0eef0ba1124e5bdab))
* **html:** an absolute child of a flex box or a grid is in none of its items, and a floated one is an item, so the chevrons of github.com/features/ai/github-app's accordion line up at the end of each row ([#804](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/804)) ([e9cf275](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/e9cf2755dfed46368e26be93992f67c3b518efa2))
* **html:** an element a page's script focuses shows its ring only where the user's last input was not a press, so a menu github.blog opens on a click draws none round its first link ([#785](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/785)) ([cd48118](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/cd481181ec75347496257e39120350b1c14d629d))
* **html:** an input button the page styles is drawn as the page has it, as a button element is, so a black pill submit button is not the palette's framed white one ([#812](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/812)) ([bfcc300](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/bfcc300bc5dd33d8182769659b94093c3e40ac0b))
* **html:** hidden="until-found" keeps an element's box and skips what it holds, and a script's animate() finishes, so a mega menu draws only the panel it opened ([#805](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/805)) ([e94b33b](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/e94b33be0c70bfd25d086ffbd6840c05f78444c5))
* **html:** mask layers are composited as mask-composite says, so a card outlined by its border box less its content box is not tinted all over ([#810](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/810)) ([881daef](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/881daefaf44eb240d30028ee31e4a69fae2f02d5))
* **html:** onParsed waits for core to lay the element out, so a page's first scripts measure the document at the width it is drawn at ([#793](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/793)) ([0bbdbee](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/0bbdbeeab7644e5e2b726b22ef8798a3d3c60cd3))
* **html:** scrollIntoView leaves the page where it is for a box fixed to the viewport, and scrolls only a container inside it, so a fixed sidebar brings its item into view without moving the page ([a18d015](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/a18d015cd944c5a0ed747fcd622ea4cbe7220734))
* **html:** scrollIntoView scrolls only the scroll containers whose scroll moves the element, so a box absolute in a wrapper around a scrolling list is brought into view by the page and leaves the list where it is ([a18d015](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/a18d015cd944c5a0ed747fcd622ea4cbe7220734))
* **reorder:** drag and drop works on a Mac, and the reorder example with it ([#803](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/803)) ([6dbe5e4](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/6dbe5e42ae175dea4fc164aab6f423ca9cebfa41))
* **three:** &lt;effectComposer&gt; draws on the native macOS backend, and a &lt;points&gt; there is drawn at its size ([#808](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/808)) ([c2baa0f](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/c2baa0fb635be69f17803e58302403a381e4e589))

## [0.24.0](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/compare/v0.23.1...v0.24.0) (2026-10-15)


### Features

* **html:** &lt;Html scripts&gt; runs a document's scripts with one prop, the engine and the network moved out of the browser example into src/html ([#776](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/776)) ([51882d4](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/51882d45063782249ac87db7890f8ba20cf0b8be))

## [0.23.1](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/compare/v0.23.0...v0.23.1) (2026-10-14)


### Bug Fixes

* **examples:** an element in the browser example can take the pointer, so a slider's track that asks hasPointerCapture at every move no longer throws, and a press is a pointerdown before it is a mousedown ([#775](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/775)) ([5ffb099](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/5ffb099b149f79fd01a48515e934831a7398d30d))
* **html:** a focus stop a clip hides is placed inside the clip at 2x as well, so the Zen Garden's home page no longer scrolls sideways into blank space ([#773](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/773)) ([b7ea48f](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/b7ea48f54fbe0ad95b9544b2f5c495b983551ad0))

## [0.23.0](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/compare/v0.22.0...v0.23.0) (2026-10-12)


### Features

* canvas paths, Path2D and gradients in the browser example; &lt;Html&gt; draws a canvas in its box ([#771](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/771)) ([a90421e](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/a90421ea50a2a96431d54ea8ede1b38667196fe6))
* **html:** a box whose overflow scrolls is a scroll container, under the wheel and a page's scripts, so a page set in a scrolling main can be read past its first screen ([#770](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/770)) ([3e537e1](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/3e537e15a7fdc77d7a174452809a4f96cb4a9587))
* **html:** a scroll container keeps its sticky boxes, takes the scroll keys and draws core's bar, which a drag moves ([#772](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/772)) ([6946447](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/6946447afdc1a777a6cd5ea64c0dbc151804d018))


### Bug Fixes

* **markdown:** a document's prose and code are set in the theme's fontFamily and monoFamily, and &lt;Code&gt;'s in its monoFamily, where they were set in sans-serif and monospace under any theme ([#762](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/762)) ([#768](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/768)) ([e1a0e03](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/e1a0e03dc42d80d74e933c769e798d9b74ed09f1))
* **rich-text-editor:** a bracket is escaped only where it could be part of a link, so [[wiki links]] and [x] are saved as they were typed, where every bracket came back escaped ([#761](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/761)) ([#767](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/767)) ([2e797ce](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/2e797ce441f5eb8d1b97247894a8d533b96a69cb))
* **richtext:** the words of an inline code span share one chip, where each word's chip was padded past its neighbour and a translucent fill showed a darker band at every space ([#763](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/763)) ([#769](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/769)) ([1fbdad1](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/1fbdad1d1488346a85ecd7e1f4944f739cd82f06))
* up.com.au loads in the browser example: WebGL context, stream bodies, clipped mounts ([#765](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/765)) ([7809057](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/7809057b655523c97d1a6f1a1f81a68a6cd36fed))

## [0.22.0](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/compare/v0.21.0...v0.22.0) (2026-10-11)


### Features

* **examples:** a page's fetch reaches another origin where CORS lets it, preflighted where it is no simple request, where every request to another origin was refused ([#743](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/743)) ([cab4e1f](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/cab4e1f0c9e17863cfbafdce96e896811c33b94a))
* **examples:** a page's scripts keep records in an IndexedDB of the document's own, and markup set as a script's or a style's innerHTML is its text, where yahoo.com's consent script was cut off at its first less-than sign ([#740](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/740)) ([d24ee61](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/d24ee617c3adb9fb744f5177deb6b360be2d9116))
* **examples:** a page's scripts read streams, attach shadow roots the document draws, and edit attributes as nodes, which the app router of Next.js hydrates with ([#738](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/738)) ([41aa12f](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/41aa12f41637475b4f8bf11e108cd17008c522a7))
* **examples:** the browser runs a page's classic scripts where its site's JS switch is on, in a node:vm context a document over a DOM of &lt;Html&gt;'s tree, and stops one that runs away ([#730](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/730)) ([4c023cf](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/4c023cf21e2eae15cfcef808ce7cbab1ed959ae8))
* **examples:** the browser runs a page's module scripts, their imports fetched through its network and linked, import.meta.url and import() from a classic script or a module ([#734](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/734)) ([6c3ffd6](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/6c3ffd6c13cdbee7f3f55ae306920b545e0fbe95))
* **examples:** the browser runs a page's workers, each in a thread of its own, and a frame of another origin at its own origin, so react.dev's Sandpack compiles and shows its examples ([#756](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/756)) ([2c8a9c4](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/2c8a9c41fd95e3253f53827b1f0300f8cc91c104))
* **examples:** the browser's pages find what sites read of the window, CSSOM over a style element's rules and adopted sheets among it, both drawn, resolve bare imports through an import map, and hear an unhandled rejection as unhandledrejection with its reason ([#735](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/735)) ([1e46a03](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/1e46a030788ab0c0b8ab96e16318d8b7f70185dc))
* **examples:** the browser's script host records each change a page makes once, for refresh(changes) and for the page's MutationObservers, adds XMLHttpRequest over fetch, and starts every tab's pane with a heap bound on react-x11 2.46.0 ([#732](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/732)) ([3b756e4](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/3b756e474f60dff4cca15e59ca16c145f35ce9dd))
* **html:** &lt;Html document&gt; draws a tree a host holds in place of parsing a source, so the browser example draws a page's frames over their boxes and runs one with a script in it in a realm of its own, linked to the page's ([#754](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/754)) ([4de95c0](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/4de95c0d16ee7ce2d7837b7b9328e91d0b5bcc4b))
* **html:** a table's rows go in the tbody HTML's parser implies, and the browser's scripts get the DOM Acid3 tests — live ranges, XML documents, frames that load and run — so it scores 96 of 100, as Chrome does ([#745](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/745)) ([8cc02b4](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/8cc02b48f11308be1e82eebe00bf276308b7c137))
* **html:** an [@font-face](https://github.com/font-face)'s size-adjust scales its face and its ascent-, descent- and line-gap-override set its lines, a local() face's included, so a fallback tuned to the room of the font it stands in for takes that room ([#724](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/724)) ([f5ce4ad](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/f5ce4ad602424b66ef6eea3344a2f50d6530a414))
* **html:** backdrop-filter draws what is painted behind a box through its blur and colour functions where react-x11's native context runs them, a radial gradient that changes slowly is drawn from a raster of it kept small, and a gradient's stops are handed over as CSS mixes them, where one fading to transparent faded through grey on macOS ([#742](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/742)) ([d181a5b](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/d181a5b4151a012903605ff6d52b82b07830c14f))
* **html:** container queries — container-type, container-name and [@container](https://github.com/container), answered for the content box the last layout gave the nearest container and asked again of what a layout moved, and the cqw, cqi and kin units ([#723](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/723)) ([8efe59a](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/8efe59ad493b62f7e21ed3edf43799cfa0aa3b50))
* **html:** if(), :target from the document URL's fragment, and percentages and angles in a colour's math ([#722](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/722)) ([f1dd338](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/f1dd33825fffe160d65dc580d459df34e198ea26))
* **html:** onDomEvent tells the pointer's moves, the wheel and a secondary press, and only what draws something covers a frame, a canvas or a player, so the browser example runs the react-x11 playground ([#755](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/755)) ([bfb8ce4](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/bfb8ce4dc447bae3d15bef0c4f27f8099bd41752))
* **html:** onViewportOverflow tells the host what overflow the root or the body gave the viewport, and the browser example hides its scrollbar and keeps the wheel and the keys off a page that hides it, as Acid2 asks ([#725](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/725)) ([11ddab5](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/11ddab5eb6214903f9263655ba0015ade3d92281))
* **html:** refresh(changes) takes MutationObserver-shaped records and restyles what a selector testing the change reaches, in place where it only inks, through a frame's build where a box out of the flow moved, and in a build keeping every other style otherwise ([#731](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/731)) ([832f584](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/832f584b536769f4827a406c8b18a92e21abb422))
* **html:** renderEmbedded mounts what a host gives over an iframe, a canvas or an embed, at its content box, so a browser can show a frame's document where the box was empty ([#752](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/752)) ([22111b9](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/22111b9ed80f57edeca639bdf69d1db0cf2fe853))
* **html:** the handle answers matchMedia as the document's own [@media](https://github.com/media) rules do, computedStyle reads color-scheme, and a &lt;link&gt;'s sheet is told as a load once a restyle has applied it, or as an error ([#739](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/739)) ([dd13e11](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/dd13e1184cb89a37e7672fced9e7bd93a16310cb))
* **html:** the handle's scriptsUnblocked settles once the style sheets before a script are in, and the browser runs a page's scripts after them, so one after a head's &lt;link&gt; reads the document as the sheet styles it ([#741](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/741)) ([5519ae2](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/5519ae24ca1678c0db8e7bb2400ecbdab24af205))
* **html:** what a host that runs a page's scripts needs of it — a scripting prop, onDomEvent told before every default and cancelled with false, a control's live value, the focus, a form's methods, onParsed and onLoaded, and computedStyle ([#729](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/729)) ([18d065c](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/18d065c01c095d4c9f60c0528e7528ff21e2cd9b))


### Bug Fixes

* **charts:** a new data array of the same length draws its own rows, where the chart kept drawing the last array's ([#760](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/760)) ([5c4debe](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/5c4debe806011998a2c6de31084b0bcd3bd65790)), closes [#759](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/759)
* **deps:** react-x11 ^2.43.0, so a surface &lt;Html&gt; draws and lets go of in one paint on Wayland is drawn as it was, where it came out an opaque black box, and a box in perspective is one quad there where it was a few hundred tiles ([#710](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/710)) ([1e2a855](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/1e2a8552c2e8c74a26627dffa935cffa751fbf38))
* **deps:** react-x11 ^2.44.1, so a page in the browser example is shown at its size while a frame of the window's new size is on its way on a Mac, where it was stretched at every step of a drag ([#715](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/715)) ([1615e33](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/1615e33ec7bf5f6e9f14821dfaa317a1a06a9fe7))
* **deps:** react-x11 ^2.47.1, so on a Mac a backdrop-filter is blurred and a radial gradient is drawn, where the backdrop was sharp and every radial gradient flat ([#751](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/751)) ([2bd792a](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/2bd792a84d74cbfac82199cad1cdadc108e25234))
* **examples:** a browser tab that is not showing is no stop in the window's Tab order, where Tab off the end of the page showing went on into the next tab's page, off to the side of the window ([#727](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/727)) ([93534c8](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/93534c860e5127d9c5dd4c226e8dd5210fc5d2f1))
* **examples:** a link, a form or window.open whose target names a frame sends the frame and not the tab, so officeworks.com.au stays on officeworks.com.au ([#748](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/748)) ([bf8c713](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/bf8c7133b7dbbf5554fb2efadbd8fefc440894dc))
* **examples:** a page's failed import() rejects with the page's own TypeError, where it rejected with the host's and reached process, and the browser runs a page's scripts only where the runtime keeps it so ([#733](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/733)) ([c3b689e](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/c3b689e7538527b16248e04bdccd7d9bb395ee27))
* **examples:** a page's own User-Agent header is dropped as Chrome drops it, so a preflight no longer names one the server never allowed ([#744](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/744)) ([94be1af](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/94be1afd5b40e2a491fe4228d51e4c83de5fae86))
* **examples:** a page's uncaught promise no longer closes its tab, import() runs on Bun, and a frame's window has a location and the page's globals, so react.dev, x.com and mazda.com.au load ([#747](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/747)) ([648bb6a](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/648bb6a77f13eb87ea94d27b782267358948222e))
* **examples:** react.dev's code editors are drawn, as what a page assigns and feature-detects matches the DOM, and the page hears its pane scroll ([#749](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/749)) ([cdb29b3](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/cdb29b315ec7cdf4c0ba5d0c04009ffbe16f85f8))
* **html:** a field with the focus that a dragged width sets aside keeps its widget where it was, so the focus and the caret stay with it, where the first step of a drag took them away ([#716](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/716)) ([f311fa4](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/f311fa4ce6f7c2e5c3dc27b9836aaba63cd8eceb))
* **html:** a form control on a page in the colour scheme the palette is not is drawn in Chrome's colours for that scheme, so a page that names no scheme, which a browser host draws light, has light controls in a dark application, where its text area and its submit button were the dark palette's widgets ([#712](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/712)) ([6892d51](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/6892d510e6ba4fb66969467a634b4d8175d91027))
* **html:** a form reset sets its fields' text in place, where it mounted every widget in the document again, so the button pressed keeps the focus and a field in another form keeps its caret and its undo ([#726](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/726)) ([8100282](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/8100282f34f907a61250645213117d150cd61c8a))
* **html:** a radial gradient drawn from a raster is drawn from one a quarter of its size each way at least, so a glow on a dark page is smooth on a Mac, where it was a mottle of squares ([#753](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/753)) ([ba7c253](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/ba7c2534313cb8a984202290eb9f301b71b05378))
* **html:** a refresh told of an attribute set to the value it had restyles nothing, where a page that wrote the same class or custom property at every frame built the document's boxes at each ([#736](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/736)) ([ea3b895](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/ea3b89515ea4974b3d19b187b914e8dffa4ff172))
* **html:** a script's src is resolved against the document's base, refresh() leaves nothing an element keeps stale, and elementAtPoint lays out first ([#728](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/728)) ([bade983](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/bade9834c7f8bbcb3d4d6b2b479fffe8df9b90ea))
* **html:** a written &lt;html&gt; gets the head HTML's parser implies, an id that is a space is a selector, and an &lt;object&gt; of a page is a frame, so Acid3's page is drawn as Chrome draws it ([#746](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/746)) ([f2c0f7d](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/f2c0f7dc0b616038ce0db24d8e136a8e17571290))
* **html:** an SVG drawn in a faded element is drawn at the element's opacity on macOS too, so reactmelbourne.com is no black window ([#750](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/750)) ([40231ae](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/40231ae4f62aeb4ae824bbfe4ed8e573e70104da))
* **html:** box-shadow runs between two lists of shadows, the shorter padded with shadows of nothing in transparent, so the Zen Garden's all-designs cards grow their glow over the quarter second they are hovered in, where the glow came at once ([#704](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/704)) ([252c821](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/252c821141e100993c86958acb80926814b0fbab))
* **html:** refresh(changes) restyles what a change reaches in a document with shadow trees whose rules stay inside them, where every change there was a refresh of everything ([#737](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/737)) ([14d4907](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/14d4907d1f7737e43fc75b925ef3787aa02d8a8b))


### Performance Improvements

* **html:** a background or an image drawn at another size than its own is copied from a raster kept at that size, a tile at a time where it is large, so a frame of a resize of Zen Garden 101 in the browser example takes 12 ms on macOS where it took 16 ([#714](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/714)) ([ccfdfb3](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/ccfdfb3bae9a887c1cb9a2d20b42f5134256c3c5))
* **html:** a box in perspective is handed to a context that draws an image through a projection as one draw, where it was drawn a tile at a time, so Zen Garden 219's tilted sidebar runs at 25 frames a second on Wayland where it ran at 12 ([#707](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/707)) ([c7891cc](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/c7891ccc7d8e8800354e3f4f05827090dcbdd850))
* **html:** a box painted on a surface keeps it across the frames of an animation inside it, and only what moved is painted again on it, where a frame's build forgot the surface and the box was painted whole ([#708](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/708)) ([0f45e7c](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/0f45e7c06c7f16bb9787d771978d1578798ccf54))
* **html:** a box's shadows in transition go on a layer of their own, drawn and blurred by the render server, while the box stays the document's, so a frame of a Zen Garden card's hover takes 12 ms on macOS where it took 22, and 4 once its glow is lifted ([#705](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/705)) ([6dfb9f2](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/6dfb9f24022dfed1b9e4dcc46a39f623707f4ea7))
* **html:** a drag makes a raster only of an image size the frame before drew too, a gradient whose stops hold one colour over a run is filled a run at a time, and a faded box drawn through its matrix keeps its group while the matrix holds, so a frame of a resize of Zen Garden 214's wide layout paints in 20 ms on a Mac where it took 32 ([#721](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/721)) ([615b041](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/615b041363cdc8cf1d06ebadfed25c456d8930a0))
* **html:** a dragged width keeps the block at the top of the pane where it is on screen and lays out only from there, and lays the document out whole once the drag ends, so a drag half way down Wikipedia's X Window System article in the browser example shows 26 frames a second where it showed 15 ([#718](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/718)) ([fa6b6b7](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/fa6b6b7d49296ac55153dd53a08c88f091081948))
* **html:** a frame of the animations waits for the window once the paint it is owed is two frames late, so on a server slower than the clock the document builds half as often, where it built frames nobody saw ([#709](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/709)) ([38fe321](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/38fe3214e590a6eb9bc1ac4722cd2198b89aa618))
* **html:** a live drag the document cannot keep up with is drawn cheaper while it moves, its scaled backgrounds on sizes 4% apart and its animations paused, and as at rest once the hand holds still or lets go, so a size a drag of Zen Garden 214 asks for in the browser example is on screen in 26 ms at the median where it took 37 ([#719](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/719)) ([06a2452](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/06a24524d9133cafad6ed0282124dc7033855a90))
* **html:** a surface kept for a box survives a layout at another width that lays the box out as it was, and one given up is drawn on again at its size, so Zen Garden 101's drag narrower and back takes 8.5 s on Wayland where it took 29 ([#713](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/713)) ([ce1b01f](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/ce1b01f115eafba74de640bf4f85aa8fd6ebae07))
* **html:** an animation asks for its next frame when what it draws next changes by an eighth of a pixel or a level of colour, and a ::before or an ::after one turns, scales or fades is restyled where it is and repainted alone, so Zen Garden 214 at rest takes 4% of a core on a Mac where it took more than one ([#720](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/720)) ([3b4369f](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/3b4369fd4c6bebcf343a9eb81f489def82554a57))
* **html:** an image that arrives after the boxes were built takes its size in place, and is painted where that moves no box, so Wikipedia's X Window System article in the browser example is built 3 times as it loads where it was built 13 to 16, and its load takes 2.1 s of the page's frames where it took 3.7 to 4.5 ([#717](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/717)) ([8249c7b](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/8249c7b67c5a23cc0c6051ccd1fe508927d9fc4b))

## [0.21.0](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/compare/v0.20.0...v0.21.0) (2026-10-04)


### Features

* **html:** a box turned out of the plane goes on a layer of its own on macOS, still or moving, and so does what is painted over it, so Zen Garden 219 draws its tilted sidebar on the GPU and runs at 54 frames a second at rest where it ran at 25 ([#672](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/672)) ([52bf4be](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/52bf4be05debf88748604884d3b2e06634e71638))
* **html:** defaultColorScheme, the scheme a page that names none is drawn in, and the browser example draws one light as every browser does ([#670](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/670)) ([cdca003](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/cdca0030da877ca210ea41a59b0b5d73aea73380))
* **html:** filter, its colour functions drawn over a group read back, and every list a stacking context and the containing block of what is out of the flow in it, so ekazinich.com's hero screenshots are grey until their card is hovered and fade to colour through its transition, where they were drawn in colour ([#679](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/679)) ([d7f5cfa](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/d7f5cfa0227ab843b5b3f13d6c1c5686649488ac))
* **html:** react-x11 ^2.38.0, so a video in a page the browser example shows in a &lt;Frame&gt; pane plays on macOS, on a player the host makes for it, where a pane ran no AppKit and every video in a page sat on its poster ([#666](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/666)) ([e6b933c](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/e6b933c6c854a5510de2f88a012223373145a6ee))
* **html:** react-x11 ^2.40.0, so on macOS a CSS filter's colour functions are run by the native context in the paint that draws the element, where its group was read back and drawn a round trip late, and a perspective's tiles are drawn at the smoothing they ask for ([#689](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/689)) ([1bb1787](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/1bb17877a043aedf6f4496c543136170f4bcc2a0))
* **html:** translate, rotate and scale are what they are in space, so `rotate: y 40deg` turns a box as rotateY(40deg) does and `translate: 0 0 100px` brings it nearer in the perspective around it, where all three dropped what was out of the plane ([#667](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/667)) ([bf0d30b](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/bf0d30b6f36575585f121176d5c8ae8ac559627d))
* **html:** user-select keeps text out of a selection, and generated content is out of one as in every browser, so a copy across a &lt;q&gt; takes no quotes and a press on a page's toolbar keeps the selection there is ([#676](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/676)) ([4c5c893](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/4c5c8937b8bc2ab9a84c8ecce850104bbaa4eb65))


### Bug Fixes

* **deps:** ntk 8.21.1, so an inline SVG's marker inside a display: none subtree is drawn nowhere and one that is display: none itself is drawn, as Chrome and WebKit draw them, where 8.21.0 had both the other way round ([#657](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/657)) ([3729fc0](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/3729fc0bc1543d63138c40b42ae707d8925fb8e5))
* **html:** `all` sets every property but direction and unicode-bidi, where it was no declaration at all, so a `button { all: unset }` takes the UA sheet's ground, border and control font off the button, and asteriskmag.com's menu button is three lines rather than a filled box ([#697](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/697)) ([4ba5b87](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/4ba5b87cd765f144439d5bf301c0cfcaf19b65c1))
* **html:** a drag that crosses a breakpoint goes on laying out only what can be seen after it, and a layout that stops measures the boxes it sizes from all they hold ([#682](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/682)) ([488ac7b](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/488ac7b969e653d78e6f68a1dd9b39b4bfbb5841))
* **html:** a drawing is cut to its clip paths, through a use in a clipPath as Illustrator writes one, so CSS Zen Garden 215's arm stays in its circle, where ntk drew no clip path and the arm reached out of it ([#675](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/675)) ([cb77ac5](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/cb77ac5f776ef9472723eb45376bcc91a911a153))
* **html:** a filtered box moving a fraction of a pixel at a time is drawn from what was read, moved with it, and read again once still, so ekazinich.com's stacked phones turn without shimmering, where reads landing between frames drew them crisp between frames drawn resampled ([#688](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/688)) ([07a8fe8](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/07a8fe88ae3c09a575c9312e332e5be5ff2a49f2))
* **html:** a paragraph whose bidi controls all open left to right is laid out as one with none, so a justified line with an isolated citation on it is justified, and Wikipedia's paragraphs are no longer ordered a piece at a time at every width of a resize ([#678](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/678)) ([5027fbf](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/5027fbfc2f756207f98a275dc744070529b52a10))
* **html:** a part on a layer keeps the frames it sampled across a build that styles its element again the same, and a build that keeps an element gives its ::before the document's time rather than the last a part sampled, so Zen Garden 214's frames across a breakpoint take 40 ms at the 95th percentile where they took 123 ([#694](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/694)) ([aab8205](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/aab82051cf0dc4f429a54ad4e8edb6b2e79dd09a))
* **html:** a press starts a selection in the text drawn on top under it, so a drag over a paragraph Zen Garden 220 paints over its large "Est. 2003" selects the paragraph, where it selected the word behind ([#674](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/674)) ([fe13324](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/fe13324dabfc4c05cf4163476ae2b6af830bf5ef))
* **html:** a streamed &lt;script&gt; is handed over whole, and a :has() answers the tree as it is after it changes; and a design for running page scripts in the browser example ([#700](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/700)) ([1e612c8](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/1e612c81d803bdd754a61dca6d60a1dab6e033bd))
* **html:** a stylesheet declined after the first rendering waits on it lets that rendering go on, so Zen Garden 215, whose two imported http: sheets a secure page's host refuses, is drawn and measured whole, where it waited for something else to ask again ([#671](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/671)) ([9f562ce](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/9f562ce6a5547577abf619d15ef9b6b3d6349b35))
* **html:** a text field's `::placeholder` is the colour its widget draws the hint in, faded by its opacity, so asteriskmag.com's newsletter field shows "Your email address" in the page's grey where it drew it in black, and a `<textarea>` shows its placeholder at all ([#701](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/701)) ([bc55eb1](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/bc55eb128787f15c4d31342050c4514f1f5f0832))
* **html:** an animation on a box fixed to the viewport repaints where the box is drawn, so Zen Garden 215's starburst turns and its robot rises while the page is still, where both moved only as a scroll repainted them ([#669](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/669)) ([dc93484](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/dc9348494e4ab892eddc74f8624f37427553f748))
* **html:** grid items are placed and painted in `order`, so the Zen Garden's about page below 800px draws its nav above the article, as Chrome does ([#696](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/696)) ([b2d29fb](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/b2d29fbb5411cd200fa364e0f5de53b715884e3f))
* **html:** the pointer over a video with controls is the arrow, as a browser's is, where it was the I-beam the document's root shows as a selectable surface ([#664](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/664)) ([fafe302](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/fafe3028b3ad77f733adf07e5b10ad8ef9813945))
* **html:** Windows — line-height: normal without a stated lineHeight, edges spaced from a line's advance, justification the engine has; and a --native Zen Garden bench ([#699](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/699)) ([a9dafa5](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/a9dafa5067adb20da9561b8a2796a8119052384f))


### Performance Improvements

* **examples:** a browser tab that is not showing is brought to the window's size once the window stops, where it was laid out again at every step of a drag, so a drag costs a hidden tab's page 30 ms of CPU where it cost 5 s ([#698](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/698)) ([58bf29e](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/58bf29e62d95fb4a35db72eef74cabcba17dc886))
* **html:** a breakpoint keeps the ::before and ::after of the elements whose styles it keeps, and a drag's first step lays out what can be seen, so Wikipedia's 1120px breakpoint costs a frame 45 ms where it cost 63 and a drag starts in 23 where it caught for 80 ([#691](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/691)) ([0b3fc52](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/0b3fc525467a252696069ceae38cef34d028e982))
* **html:** a breakpoint restyles the elements the rules it turns over match, and under them only what their changed styles change, and a drag across one lays out what can be seen, so Wikipedia's 1120px breakpoint costs a frame 63 ms where it cost 230 and its 1400px one 15 ([#690](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/690)) ([6386d74](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/6386d7421f8a273f9d5ca9daddc1f6b1318ff02e))
* **html:** a document whose canvas is opaque says so to core, which fills nothing under it, so a frame of a resize of Wikipedia's article fills the window one and a half times where it filled it three and a half ([#681](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/681)) ([f10e5b3](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/f10e5b394a6850252ac1aa7b4f47dcf2847e6998))
* **html:** a dragged window edge lays a document out down to a viewport past what can be seen, and the whole of it once the width rests, so Wikipedia's X Window System article reflows at every step of a drag in some 10 ms where each step took 60 ([#680](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/680)) ([5aa10ae](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/5aa10aed10b3e2998534120ac410e780734ff8c3))
* **html:** a flex layout frees the nodes it made in Yoga itself, so a page of 800 cards lays out in 104 ms where it took 115 ([#661](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/661)) ([10260e5](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/10260e593d32ce42be4557673cfb605cb0049057))
* **html:** a justified line beside a float is set from its own text, where the rest of its paragraph was laid out again for each line, so Zen Garden 005 lays a frame of a resize out in 16 ms where it took 30 ([#693](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/693)) ([25b5e8b](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/25b5e8b70c2e18fa59ce6146418c1ed998178ecd))
* **html:** a justified paragraph is laid out once, justified by the text engine where it says it can, where it took three layouts and CoreText shaped it again at every width, so Zen Garden 001 lays a frame of a resize out in 2.7 ms where it took 14 ([#695](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/695)) ([fe16f41](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/fe16f41ef9176f630cd291ed688a7f1aa4fefffa))
* **html:** a large linear gradient straight down or across is shaded as a strip 64 pixels wide and copied along, so a page's body gradient costs a frame that repaints most of the window 1.7 ms on macOS where it cost 6 ([#703](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/703)) ([8e9af6e](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/8e9af6e463f1b1886aafca71619389dffc1a9864))
* **html:** a resize restyles a document only where a style holds a length of the viewport's, so Wikipedia's article, whose skin mentions a 75vw no element of it takes, is laid out again at each width rather than styled and built again first ([#677](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/677)) ([5b87072](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/5b87072e3ff1814c225dacf3dd039f37aff8123f))
* **html:** a rule that is its class or its id alone matches its bucket without the matcher, and one that names an ancestor no ancestor has is passed over without a climb to the root, so a restyle under 2,000 utility rules takes 379 ms where it took 640 ([#660](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/660)) ([44e53a5](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/44e53a54ae43afaa1e486f234e042bd76c1dd930))
* **html:** a small background image repeated is drawn from a block of its tiles kept on a surface, so a Zen Garden design that lays a GIF of a few pixels across its boxes paints a frame of a resize in 4 to 9 ms where it took 20 to 30, and a box that takes more than 4096 tiles is covered where the image was drawn once ([#692](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/692)) ([fff94ec](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/fff94ecb4d3a00b3b9e4c4339be9528b67ff8583))
* **html:** a stylesheet the head links to holds the first rendering, and an image that is only painted is painted where it lands, so a Zen Garden design arriving over a network is built once where it was built some ten times ([#658](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/658)) ([1907d76](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/1907d76ce958ecd79b73bbe2bec4f02efcc27ba5))
* **html:** an outer shadow is cut away from a rounded box by bands of whole pixels, and an image drawn small is copied from a raster kept at its size, so a frame of a card's hover on the Zen Garden's all-designs page takes 27 ms on macOS where it took 51 ([#702](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/702)) ([c6f9444](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/c6f94440b3be43a8edd2b2d1c54174581a169c57))
* **html:** an SVG image drawn again at its size is copied from a raster of it, so Zen Garden 219 paints a frame of its hovers in 1.5–3.4 ms where it took 12–17, its drawings set from their paths at every paint ([#662](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/662)) ([a62f6f6](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/a62f6f6f5e50e0f2697b17b556ce84bbe452717b))
* **html:** the tiles a box in perspective is drawn in ask for bilinear smoothing, so on macOS each tile costs its own pixels where the context's own medium quality resampled the whole surface, and Zen Garden 219's sidebar hover paints in 26 ms where it took 62 ([#673](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/673)) ([9277b52](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/9277b5286b9a347fe8165117121e7c2a72f11b9a))

## [0.20.0](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/compare/v0.19.0...v0.20.0) (2026-10-03)


### Features

* **html:** a &lt;picture&gt;'s chosen &lt;source&gt; sizes its &lt;img&gt; with its width and height, and a lazy image's sizes="auto" is the width it is laid out at, where the &lt;img&gt;'s own attributes sized it and auto was passed over ([#639](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/639)) ([6df77a9](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/6df77a94575d06a3c224f4566fc95d066ad63e8f))
* **html:** a &lt;select&gt;'s list is the platform's own menu where the backend drops one, styled or not, as Safari and Chrome drop an NSMenu on macOS, where a select the page styled kept core's drawn menu ([#652](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/652)) ([1845993](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/18459936510322c3297c18f029109bf50aac6c75))
* **html:** a &lt;template shadowrootmode&gt; is its host's shadow tree, drawn with the host's children in its slots and styled by its own sheets, :host, ::slotted() and ::part(), where since a template's content became inert a component's markup drew nothing ([#638](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/638)) ([5b3eee1](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/5b3eee1bd9653348ec7ed252230f1aef94faf80b))
* **html:** a transform out of the plane is drawn in the perspective of the box it is laid out in, so the Zen Garden's 219 tilts its sidebar's panel away as Chrome does, where rotateY() and perspective were read and nothing of them drawn ([#655](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/655)) ([f932289](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/f932289db1842d79ff7d12d8132e468ae78c0af6))
* **html:** a video is core's &lt;video&gt; mounted over its box, playing what the host answers a kind 'video' request with, a src the platform's player opens or a VideoFrames sink it feeds, where a video was only ever its poster ([#645](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/645)) ([5f17924](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/5f17924fdd00414a47fe37e00a0b9ab0c2261047))
* **html:** an &lt;img srcset&gt; or an &lt;img&gt; in a &lt;picture&gt; asks for the source HTML chooses for the viewport and the display's scale, where &lt;Html&gt; asked for the src alone and an &lt;img srcset&gt; with no src showed nothing ([#635](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/635)) ([fb8f6b8](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/fb8f6b8e984cec16d33e3bcec4ad55cf31e2c415))
* **html:** an element and its ::before that both animate go on layers together, the ::before in its element's, so a button that pulses with a spinner drawn by its ::before runs in the render server, where the two went one at a time and both stayed on the document's clock ([#649](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/649)) ([866a021](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/866a0219ea748ea1df00f32aa3a57082612c2987))
* **html:** an image-set() is the one option the display's scale chooses, asked for alone and drawn at its resolution as a background, a list marker or generated content, where &lt;Html&gt; read it as an image and drew nothing for it and asked for nothing ([#640](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/640)) ([190bb3b](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/190bb3b4dbbfaf78a231a8b1f58d46ef4eb78375))
* **html:** prefers-reduced-motion follows the desktop's setting, live, as a browser follows it, and a reducedMotion prop overrides it, so a desktop that has asked for less motion gets a page's reduce branch and a switch in its settings restyles the document, where every desktop got the animations a page keeps under no-preference ([#647](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/647)) ([2f3d3b2](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/2f3d3b25ed862022bc6e1312a0fb57e8f3595db6))
* **html:** react-x11 ^2.33.0, so an element that reaches a rounded box's corners is lifted with its layer cut by them, and a shimmer sliding through a card with a radius runs in the render server, where a rounded box kept it on the document's clock ([#641](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/641)) ([6b3bd8f](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/6b3bd8fc973c68e48bb03802f966b13408a37a0e))
* **html:** react-x11 ^2.34.0, so an element animating inside one that goes on a layer goes on a layer inside that one's, and a spinner turning in a card that pulses runs in the render server, where every frame of it painted the card's layer again ([#646](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/646)) ([ef4786d](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/ef4786d4b61dfd45cc24c297e2e53e0bbb98dc67))
* **html:** react-x11 ^2.35.0, so a video whose host answers with a src plays with AVFoundation on macOS, where useSupports for mediaPlayback was false on every Mac and each video in a document showed its poster ([#650](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/650)) ([b3af8ed](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/b3af8ed1ca5951dc53b9357c65a0e0b7750a4bda))
* **html:** react-x11 ^2.36.0, so the parts of a document that a later part of its own overlaps go on layers with it, listed in the order the document paints them, and the rows of a list that slide in one into the next run in the render server on macOS, where every row but the last stayed on the document's clock ([#656](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/656)) ([10f7583](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/10f75833a6ef89968c88943deca70514164e9d85))
* **html:** text-wrap: pretty keeps a paragraph's last word off a line of its own where Chrome keeps it off, its last four lines broken again as Blink's score line breaker breaks them, where pretty wrapped as auto does and joshwcomeau.com's paragraphs ended on a word or an emoji alone ([#644](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/644)) ([c8b172b](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/c8b172b06222093de887da1adf76d107f5a2c9d1))


### Bug Fixes

* **deps:** ntk 8.21.0, so a document's inline SVG draws its markers, centres a label its text-anchor centres and sets one in its weight, where every arrow was a bare line and every label started at its anchor in the regular face ([#653](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/653)) ([05f41cd](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/05f41cdf1604cdb06c684242bf42ae2c9887ac18))
* **html:** a &lt;link&gt; or a &lt;style&gt; applies where its media attribute holds, read as an [@import](https://github.com/import)'s media queries are, so a phone sheet under max-width: 600px styles no desktop window and one under not all and min-width: 640px applies short of 640px, restyling as a resize crosses either, where a query that began with a parenthesis held at every width and one that began with not at none ([#642](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/642)) ([384cc8f](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/384cc8fa36623286e73c4fa70b0c734a10b6fc00))
* **html:** a drawing's text is set in its box's font, a mask in it is drawn on the document's surfaces, and a turned drawing with text in it is drawn on a surface, where its text was set in 16px sans-serif, its masks had no surface on macOS and a turned badge's words stayed level ([#648](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/648)) ([747b38f](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/747b38f5f081bdb2289566f690b8912b589a90dd))
* **html:** a media query reads Media Queries 4's or and not, so an or holds where any of its features does and a not over a width holds on the other side of it, where an or held at every width and a not over a width at none ([#636](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/636)) ([d807dd8](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/d807dd805f67049ca3ebf16b9bb148d6e6b1d382))
* **html:** a pseudo-element is its element to the pointer, so a panel the Zen Garden's 219 slides down the cable it hangs from keeps its :hover as it does in Chrome, where the cable named nothing and the panel lost the hover a tenth of a second after it left the pointer, slid back under it and went round again ([#654](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/654)) ([2d54e14](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/2d54e145ee188617e2f7491f560eddf8ad15273f))
* **html:** prefers-reduced-motion is answered as a desktop browser answers it, no-preference and not reduce, under animate={false} as well, so a page that keeps its animations under no-preference runs them, where it answered neither and the animations joshwcomeau.com keeps there never ran ([#643](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/643)) ([a288968](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/a288968b541e4cd52992f8b6d2e177334f95cd19))
* the animations a component runs itself follow the desktop, so Flow's marching dashes hold still while the window is minimized or the desktop asks for less motion, and the carets of CodeEditor, RichTextEditor and the vt Terminal blink at the desktop's rate or not at all where it asks for a still caret, where they blinked at the renderer's rate whatever it said and the dashes marched on a hidden window ([#651](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/651)) ([c8be7f3](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/c8be7f31ffebac6062fe77c2260ae597eecf5b41))

## [0.19.0](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/compare/v0.18.0...v0.19.0) (2026-10-02)


### Features

* **html:** a ::before or an ::after whose animation a layer can carry is lifted onto a layer of its own, as a spinner drawn by one is, where a pseudo-element's animation ran on the document's clock and kept its element's there too ([#621](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/621)) ([e1b50dd](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/e1b50dda90e245458e68b2b2e1346e426c236aa3))
* **html:** a transition of the opacity or the transform runs in the render server on macOS, lifted onto a layer as an animation of them is, where a card that faded and lifted under the pointer painted the document at every frame of it ([#629](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/629)) ([3369f71](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/3369f719ed06b0b0f51c694857e272a7c9e96ff3))
* **html:** an element fixed to the viewport, or in one, has its animation run in the render server on macOS on a layer that stays where the viewport is, so a toast fading over a page that scrolls paints no frame, where a fixed element stayed on the document's clock ([#633](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/633)) ([cdf9a78](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/cdf9a7892e90fa58954a7d16262f75d477402aa8))
* **html:** an element with a fade and a turn, two animations, is lifted with both on its layer, each with its own timing, where only an element with one animation was and the rest ran on the document's clock ([#620](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/620)) ([cae3af7](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/cae3af7baa91e100f59faf9f918ce3b98834eb36))
* **html:** CSS transitions run, from the style the document draws to the one a hover, a focus or a breakpoint makes, turning back and stopping as CSS Transitions says, where every such change went over at once ([#623](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/623)) ([99baa1a](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/99baa1a58b5a4174913678015f72f39eb9a6dbb6))
* **html:** react-x11 ^2.30.0, so an element under a box that clips it is lifted with its layer cut to the box's clip, and a carousel's slide, a marquee or a shimmer under overflow: hidden runs in the render server, where a clipping box kept it on the document's clock ([#622](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/622)) ([9d49434](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/9d494349add4bafb8736795d6c662d49ba83270f))
* **html:** react-x11 ^2.32.0, so a WebP shows in a document, decoded by the ladder core's &lt;image&gt; uses, and under Bun every format Bun.Image reads is decoded off the JavaScript thread, where &lt;Html&gt; decoded through ntk's PNG and JPEG alone and a WebP was an empty frame ([#625](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/625)) ([c943122](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/c943122afd2ae64532781dad3e27ada599a4b38d))
* **html:** will-change makes the stacking context and the containing blocks a value of what it names would, and an animation acts as though it named what it sets, so on macOS an element is lifted over whatever the document paints before it, where a toast over text or a block sliding over the paragraph after it ran on the document's clock ([#618](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/618)) ([0706ae2](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/0706ae2c615366750e91573ba5d266e0a0c1eaa4))


### Bug Fixes

* **html:** a &lt;noscript&gt; draws its children, as a browser with scripting off draws them, where the UA sheet hid it as one running JavaScript does ([#616](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/616)) ([4f0425e](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/4f0425eef4ef3cbf94b7a1b4bf9a462d7c3db691))
* **html:** a field the page set a border or a background on is the page's to draw, whatever it set them to, where border: none and background: transparent read as a field left alone and joshwcomeau.com's newsletter field was the palette's framed one, 20px taller than Chrome's ([#628](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/628)) ([29e1881](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/29e1881762b6afb59711c6e0375823380b911c35))
* **html:** a field the page set one border or background property on takes Chrome's UA edges for the rest, a text field's 2px inset border on the field's ground, where it was drawn with no frame at all and a field that took a border colour on focus lost its frame as it was focused ([b229745](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/b229745997b2e9ee7a59ce42e12a5f461dfaeca6))
* **html:** a form, a label and the document's language find nothing inside a &lt;template&gt;, whose content is inert, where a form attribute or a label's for named an element in one, its &lt;base target&gt; was the submission's, its radios joined the page's groups, a label took the control in one for its own and its content-language meta set the page's language ([#619](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/619)) ([edc715e](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/edc715e740eee8dcacd57b4b7ec4c0cc67cd9103))
* **html:** a pre is HTML's, with no padding, scroll or size of its own, and the generic monospace is set smaller where a browser sets it smaller, from a keyword or a size relative to one and never under a length, where a pre was padded and set at 0.9em and every code block on joshwcomeau.com was 20px taller than Chrome's ([#631](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/631)) ([e771980](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/e771980530195ba46f0a4004ddd04031b274a823))
* **html:** a property an animation sets starts no transition, so a spinner or a pulse with a transition on what it animates runs as written, where a transition started from the frame before at every frame and the animation trailed it ([#626](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/626)) ([d57d80f](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/d57d80f0873553e8f60f417c8c38ff4eb8698059))
* **html:** a var() in an SVG presentation attribute is the custom property it names, where SvgView read the attribute as written, and a callout's corner in var(--color-page-background) was drawn black on macOS and missing on X11, and so were a page's clouds ([#624](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/624)) ([23b4779](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/23b47796e615e6ac47059be8d8d7295db17b1118))
* **html:** an absolute box between two offsets is laid out in the height they leave, so a grid or a flex box in one aligns what it holds down it and a percentage height in it is of that height, where the box was stretched only after its content was laid out and joshwcomeau.com's play icons sat at the top of their buttons ([#627](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/627)) ([521282a](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/521282acb45f2a596033aec83e09b72b0b6a2014))
* **html:** an inline box casts its box-shadow from each of its fragments, sliced where it breaks as one unbroken box and whole round each where it is cloned, where an inline box drew no shadow and joshwcomeau.com's links, underlined by one, were plain bold text ([#634](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/634)) ([a042385](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/a04238586852f8010e14d3cd7d6579884176e8f4))
* **html:** the document's scan does not go into a &lt;template&gt;, whose content is inert, where a &lt;style&gt; in one styled the page, its images and sheets were asked for through onResource, its scripts were handed to onScript and its &lt;title&gt; and &lt;base&gt; were the document's ([#617](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/617)) ([c02156d](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/c02156de7623de9c931fe48ec328461fa3145c92))


### Performance Improvements

* **html:** a lifted element's frames are kept while its style and its animations are, so a spinner turning in a card that pulses on a layer costs 0.06 ms a frame to offer, where every frame of it sampled the card's whole cycle again, 4.8 ms ([#630](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/630)) ([2d0134d](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/2d0134d6834d548136963a5af64ae1f3c4c48b09))
* **html:** react-x11 ^2.29.0, so on macOS every app runs a CSS fade or turn a layer can carry in the render server, where on an older core nothing asked for it and the document painted every frame ([#614](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/614)) ([755cbf1](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/755cbf13c2af9e5fcef214dcd1f5b8e0827fce3b))

## [0.18.0](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/compare/v0.17.0...v0.18.0) (2026-10-02)


### Features

* **html:** an element whose CSS animation a layer can carry is offered to react-x11's presenter as a sprite, its frames sampled as the document runs them, so on macOS a fade or a turn runs in the render server at no JavaScript frame, where every frame restyled and painted the document ([#609](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/609)) ([9a64723](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/9a64723b1a3ffa6a51faaf7ebaada9f8770f2ec4))
* **html:** clip-path: polygon() cuts a box and all it holds to its vertices, by its fill rule, where a polygon was read and cut nothing ([#596](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/596)) ([fd26975](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/fd269755ca8d9212174d50429140dd28f7c0b5e1))
* **html:** run CSS animations, interpolating computed values between keyframes on the document's timeline, where every animation was drawn at rest ([#584](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/584)) ([f52b1cb](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/f52b1cb65e1469e1eb0adf4ccd0ea747be6b7c9b))
* **html:** Tab goes through a document's links, buttons and summaries in the order the markup has them, and the one it reaches is the document's :focus, ringed as the page styles it, where Tab passed over the document as one stop and nothing in it showed the focus ([#593](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/593)) ([025283f](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/025283fd4819eb35264aa3db7d377eb30d083dfc))
* **html:** text is set at the width its font-stretch asks for, on a variable face's width axis and among a family's faces of several widths, where font-stretch was not read and bun.sh's headings were set half as wide again as Chrome sets them ([#600](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/600)) ([9f70e62](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/9f70e629812fd8b8e1766a16a9ae97fc1f258abb))


### Bug Fixes

* **html:** a body with no html around it takes its percentage height of the viewport, where html and body at 100% left a page that starts &lt;!DOCTYPE html&gt;&lt;title&gt; as tall as its content ([#603](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/603)) ([433f912](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/433f9125f8daaadcece5e0ea223e316e9f9d5aba))
* **html:** a box that clips and a table are painted in the passes of the flow around them, where each was painted whole at the lines' turn, over the background of a block after it that a negative margin drew up over it ([#607](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/607)) ([0c467ad](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/0c467ad63c2959fedf8ff768929c1831c36e14fe))
* **html:** a column that wraps fits an item again into its line where the line is wider than the column, where it kept the width it had in the column ([#588](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/588)) ([9cdbd40](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/9cdbd408076f1ab6cfdaa94568621d562d243e9f))

* **html:** a function ends its component value, so a minified sheet's transform, shadow, background and grid tracks are read, where whatever followed a function's closing paren with no space was one word with it and the declaration was dropped ([#594](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/issues/594)) ([269969e](https://github.com/sidorares/react-x11-components/commit/269969eaf24cf414a5f94728fc628788d50ea566))
